     1→# MTQΣ Pilot Command Center — Work Log
     2→
     3→Project: MTQΣ Pilot Command Center (full testnet pilot portal for users)
     4→Blueprint: MTQΣ_Blueprint.docx (Source of Truth v1.2 — Final Closed-Loop Monetary Architecture)
     5→Deployed contracts: Monad Testnet (10143), Arc Testnet (5042002), Solana devnet
     6→Status: CANDIDATE FOR PUBLIC TESTING — NOT PRODUCTION-AUTHORIZED
     7→
     8→---
     9→Task ID: 1
    10→Agent: Orchestrator (COO/CTO/CFO/PM)
    11→Task: Read blueprint, define architecture, seed worklog
    12→
    13→Work Log:
    14→- Extracted MTQΣ_Blueprint.docx via pandoc (3270 lines).
    15→- Analyzed all 16 sections: GFB Index, MTQ price, Reserve NAV/RR/LCR, Asset Registry, Adaptive Macro Engine, Rebalancing Engine, Dynamic Buffer, Oracle architecture, Execution/Slippage, Geopolitical Eject, Mint/Redeem, Genesis, Governance state machine, Honest Status.
    16→- Confirmed contracts already deployed by user on Monad Testnet (10143), Arc Testnet (5042002), and Solana devnet (mint GAGRdrY6...).
    17→- Decided deliverable: a Next.js "Pilot Command Center" — a single-route web app that:
    18→  * Implements the full blueprint math (GFB Index, MTQ price, NAV, RR, LCR, buffer states, rebalancing decision, geopolitical eject) as a TypeScript reference engine.
    19→  * Reads live FX rates (ECB/Frankfurter free API, no key) and computes the GFB Index on-chain-equivalent.
    20→  * Lets pilot users simulate Mint (deposit USDC → MTQ minted = X*(1-0.001)/P_MTQ) and Redeem (burn MTQ → basket breakdown + fee 0.15%).
    21→  * Visualizes reserve composition, buffer layers, adaptive macro engine (VIX/DXY z-scores), and the 5-state risk state machine.
    22→  * Shows the deployed contract registry for all 3 testnets with explorer links + copy buttons.
    23→  * Logs every pilot trial to SQLite (Prisma) for auditability.
    24→  * Streams live metric updates via a WebSocket mini-service (port 3003).
    25→- Set up todos (12 items).
    26→
    27→Stage Summary:
    28→- Architecture: Next.js 16 App Router, single `/` route, Tailwind/shadcn, Prisma/SQLite, WebSocket mini-service on 3003.
    29→- Honest constraints: We do NOT have on-chain ABIs for the deployed contracts and testnet RPC/explorer reliability is uncertain. Therefore the pilot is a faithful reference implementation of the blueprint math that ALSO surfaces the real deployed addresses for verification. This is the honest, robust approach — it does not pretend to call contracts we cannot reliably read.
    30→- Branding theme: deep monetary/financial dark theme with gold accents (matches gold-backed design), sticky footer, fully responsive.
    31→
    32→---
    33→Task ID: 3
    34→Agent: Orchestrator (CTO)
    35→Task: Decide live-data transport architecture under sandbox constraints
    36→
    37→Work Log:
    38→- Built WebSocket mini-service (mini-services/mtq-feed) on port 3003 (socket.io path "/").
    39→- Verified the service boots, computes GFB index (1.0544), and the engine.io handshake responds correctly.
    40→- Discovered: the sandbox reaps any process spawned inside a Bash tool call when that call completes. The mini-service stays alive only within a single long-lived call; it dies at call boundary (setsid/nohup/disown do not survive).
    41→- The Next.js dev server (port 3000) persists because it is the sandbox's primary managed process.
    42→
    43→Stage Summary:
    44→- DECISION (CTO): Pivot the live market engine to run IN-PROCESS inside the persistent Next.js server, using a global singleton (globalThis-guarded) + setInterval to advance the macro/rebalance/buffer state every 4s. The frontend polls /api/metrics every 4s for live updates.
    45→- The WebSocket mini-service files are retained as a documented artifact that works in a normal long-running environment, but the pilot app does NOT depend on them, so it is robust in this sandbox.
    46→- Honest trade-off: polling at 4s instead of push WS gives ~4s freshness (excellent for a pilot dashboard) and eliminates a class of reliability failures. This is the right call for pilot stability.
    47→- The full blueprint math engine (src/lib/mtq/engine.ts) is unchanged and remains the single source of truth; only the host process changed.

   103→- Known issues / honest notes:
   104→  * The page is a client component, so SSR renders only the skeleton + footer (everything else requires /api/metrics which runs only on the client). This is intentional per the spec ("Show a tasteful skeleton while the first fetch is in-flight"). After hydration the full dashboard appears within ~1 round-trip.
   105→  * VIX & DXY are labelled as "simulated pilot signals" in three places (header FX row, MacroEngine, footer) — never misrepresented as live.
   106→  * Reserve Ratio / LCR show "∞ — fully reserved" when null or non-finite (no circulating supply yet) per the spec.
   107→  * Trial log will be empty on a fresh DB until a pilot user runs a Mint or Redeem simulation; the empty state is handled.
   108→  * Recharts is a client-only library — its components are only used inside 'use client' presentational components, so no SSR issues.

   131→Task ID: A1
   132→Agent: Orchestrator (COO/CTO/CFO/PM + tokenomics)
   133→Task: Line-by-line audit of blueprint v1.2 vs current implementation; gap inventory
   134→
   135→Work Log:
   136→- Re-extracted MTQΣ_Blueprint.docx (identical v1.2, Σ-v1.2, date 2026-09-03, md5 c3a0de557801d0318abb2192803d1ec4).
   137→- Audited current src/lib/mtq/engine.ts + blueprint.ts against every section §1–§16.
   138→
   139→Gap inventory (line-by-line):
   140→1. §9 Oracle Architecture — MISSING. Blueprint requires 3 sources (Chainlink/Pyth/Chronicle), staleness 60s, confidence interval <1%, deviation <2.5% from median, median-of-3 (or average-of-2), PAUSE minting+rebalancing if <2 feeds valid. Current code uses Frankfurter directly with no consensus/validation layer.
   141→2. §5 Asset Admission Registry — PARTIAL. Blueprint requires: 8 eligibility criteria, 4 asset states (ACTIVE/WATCH/RESTRICTED/EJECTED), currency→asset mapping (USD→USDC, EUR→EURC, GBP/JPY/CNY→TBD), concentration limit 30% per issuer (warn at 25%), 48h timelock add. Current code has haircuts only.
   142→3. §11.3 Reintegration Score — MISSING. Blueprint: R_score = w1·TimeInBand(48h) + w2·LiquidityDepth + w3·OracleAgreement + w4·(1−Volatility), threshold >0.80, staged re-purchase 25%→50%→100%. Current code has eject ladder but no reintegration.
   143→4. §13.2 Treasury Sweep — MISSING. Blueprint: hot wallet surplus stablecoins >$10,000 → sweep to 4/7 Multi-Sig Cold Treasury. Current code: nothing.
   144→5. §8.5 First-Loss Waterfall — PARTIAL (logic exists, not surfaced). Blueprint: 5-layer waterfall Operational Surplus → Buffer Fiat → Buffer Gold → Core Fiat → Core Gold. Need to track + surface current layer + consumed amounts.
   145→6. §3.6 PriceUpdated events — MISSING. Blueprint: emit PriceUpdated when GFB changes >0.5%. Need event log.
   146→7. §12.2 vs §3.4.2 CONTRADICTION (honest find): §3.4.2 says RedeemValue = Y×P_MTQ (index-priced); §12.2 says Value = Y×NAV_t where NAV_t=V_net/S (book value per token). These conflict: at RR=110%, NAV/S = 1.10×P_MTQ, so §12.2 would pay redeemers 10% MORE than §3.4.2, draining the buffer surplus via arbitrage. As tokenomics expert: §3.4.2 is the arbitrage-safe, economically correct version; §12.2 is likely a documentation error. Honest decision: implement P_MTQ as primary, compute both, flag the contradiction in a dedicated "Honest Audit" UI section.
   147→8. §10.1 Quote-based execution — PARTIAL. Blueprint: fetch route quote from 1inch/Paraswap before swap; two-sided bounds. Current: slippage tolerance only. Will add a quote simulation.
   148→9. UI/UX: user requests "state of art unique UI architecture, one of a kind full structure" → full bespoke redesign required beyond generic shadcn cards.
   149→
   150→Stage Summary:
   151→- 9 gaps identified; 7 engine gaps + 1 honest contradiction + 1 UI/UX rebuild.
   152→- Decision (CTO): implement engine gaps myself (correctness-critical, exact math), then dispatch bespoke UI rebuild to frontend subagent.
   153→- Decision (COO): ship as one cohesive "Monetary Observatory" pilot — engine v2 then UI v2, verify end-to-end.

   693→- The new v1.0 Master blueprint is a FUNDAMENTAL architectural change from v1.2. It supersedes ALL prior versions (v1.0 archived, v1.1 archived, v1.2 superseded by v2.0 which is issued as Master v1.0).
   694→
   695→FULL AUDIT — 13 major gap areas:
   696→
   697→1. BASKET (MAJOR): v1.2 fixed q_i (5 currencies, no gold) → v1.0 adaptive W_t (7 components: USD/EUR/JPY/GBP/CNY/CHF/Gold). Gold is now IN the index. CHF added. Strategic prior: USD 27%, EUR 20%, JPY 9%, GBP 8%, CNY 5%, CHF 5%, Gold 26%.
   698→
   699→2. WEIGHT STATES (MAJOR): v1.2 single target → v1.0 four states: Strategic Prior (soft anchor), Target (MASE output), Smoothed (stress-adaptive), Execution (MARP trade decision). W^Prior ≠ W^Target ≠ W^Smooth ≠ W^Execution.
   700→
   701→3. MASE ENSEMBLE (MAJOR): v1.2 single macro engine (VIX/DXY → θ ±3%) → v1.0 MASE: ensemble of 5+ models (minimum-variance, ERC/risk-parity, max-diversification, CVaR/tail-risk, purchasing-power, regime) with adaptive ensemble weights.
   702→
   703→4. GOLD IN INDEX (MAJOR): v1.2 gold NOT in GFB Index → v1.0 gold IS in the index as first-class component with independent adaptive allocation W_{G,t}^Target = f(prior, risk, diversification, inflation, crisis, liquidity, purchasing-power). Envelope: 20-32%.
   704→
   705→5. CONSTITUENCY ENGINE (MAJOR): v1.2 fixed 5 currencies → v1.0 eligibility engine Q_i over expandable universe (CHF, CAD, AUD, SGD algorithmically decided via IMF COFER + BIS Triennial scoring).
   706→
   707→6. CHAIN-LINKED INDEX (MODERATE): v1.2 simple normalization → v1.0 chain-linked: NAV_t = G_t × Σ W_{i,t} × (P_{i,t}/P_{i,0}) with divisor adjustment G_t for weight changes.
   708→
   709→7. MARP (MAJOR): v1.2 simple trigger + 24h direction lock → v1.0 MARP: daily calculation vs actual rebalancing, urgency test, no-trade zones, cost-benefit gate, partial corrections, natural cash-flow preference, 6-level hierarchy.
   710→
   711→8. ENVELOPES (MAJOR): v1.2 gold clamped 22-30% → v1.0 per-component admissibility envelopes: USD 23-32%, EUR 17-24%, JPY 7-12%, GBP 6-11%, CNY 3-7%, CHF 3-7%, Gold 20-32%. Dynamic admissibility + weight-velocity limits.
   712→
   713→9. GOLD PRICE (MODERATE): v1.2 PAXG/LBMA min → v1.0 canonical multi-source gold price (robust median, confidence scoring, degraded mode, independent-source quorum).
   714→
   715→10. RESERVE VS INDEX GOLD (MAJOR): v1.2 reserve gold = core 20% + buffer 10%×62.5% → v1.0 reserve gold and index gold are MANDATORILY SEPARATE. Reserve gold sized by obligations/liquidity/custody/redemption risk, NOT by index weight.
   716→
   717→11. VALIDATION (MAJOR): v1.2 Monte Carlo 10,300 runs → v1.0 full research program: backtest from 2010, walk-forward, purged CV, Monte Carlo, perturbation, stress suite — production precondition.
   718→
   719→12. TRANSPARENCY (MODERATE): v1.2 genesis verification → v1.0 full weight publication with source data, methodology version, rebalancing decision logs, deterministic reproducibility.
   720→
   721→13. CHF (MINOR): v1.2 no CHF → v1.0 CHF is 5% strategic prior, 3-7% envelope, first-class component.

   754→### Phase 3 — engine.ts (the big rewrite)
   755→- Fixed the import block: previously imported `FxRates` from `./fx` (which never exported it — SWC was silently treating the type as `any`). Now `FxRates` is imported from `./blueprint` (where the v1.0 interface lives, including `CHF_USD` and `XAU_USD`); `FxSnapshot` is imported from `./fx`. Replaced `Q_USD, Q_EUR, Q_GBP, Q_JPY, Q_CNY` with `STRATEGIC_PRIOR`. All legacy `BASE_GOLD_WEIGHT`, `GOLD_WEIGHT_LOWER/UPPER`, `ALPHA`, `BETA`, `THETA_MAX`, `LAMBDA_*`, `BUFFER_*`, `RAMP_DURATION_HOURS` imports are RETAINED — the §6/§7/§8 buffer/MARP path still uses them (kept verbatim per task instructions).
   756→- Added `chf: number` to the `ReserveState` interface.
   757→- Rewrote `initReserveState(goldPrice)`: the genesis deposit ($1.1M) is now split across the 7 Strategic Prior components weighted by `STRATEGIC_PRIOR.{USD,EUR,JPY,GBP,CNY,CHF,Gold}` (USD 27% · EUR 20% · JPY 9% · GBP 8% · CNY 5% · CHF 5% · Gold 26%). USD is still split 1/3 each across USDC/USDP/USDT; gold is still split 50/50 across PAXG/XAUT. CHF tokens are computed at the base fixing 0.88. RR remains 1.10 (deposit/supply = 1.1M/1M).
   758→- Rewrote `computeGfbIndex()`: now takes `Pick<FxRates, "EUR_USD" | "GBP_USD" | "JPY_USD" | "CNY_USD" | "CHF_USD" | "XAU_USD">` and computes the chain-linked numerator as `Σ W^Prior_i × P_i,t` over all 7 components (USD uses 1.0, others use their FX rate, Gold uses XAU_USD). Denominator is `GFB_BASE_DENOMINATOR` (the v1.0 chain-linked denominator from blueprint.ts, which already includes Gold + CHF at base fixings).
   759→- Rewrote `reserveAssetValues()`: added `chfGross = s.chf * fx.CHF_USD`, `chfNet = chfGross * (1 - HAIRCUTS.CHF)`. Both `fiatGross` and `fiatNet` now include CHF. `nav = usdNet + eurNet + gbpNet + jpyNet + cnyNet + chfNet + goldNet`. The returned object now has `chfGross, chfNet`.
   760→- Rewrote `applyRedeem()`: the redemption basket now has 6 fiat rows (USD, EUR, JPY, GBP, CNY, CHF). The basket split uses `STRATEGIC_PRIOR` renormalised to the 6-component non-gold total (0.74) — `w = (k) => STRATEGIC_PRIOR[k] / 0.74`. Gold is still released 50/50 PAXG+XAUT, and `s.chf` is debited on redeem.
   761→- Updated `MetricsSnapshot.reserve` interface to include `chfNet`. `computeSnapshot()` now passes `chfNet: vals.chfNet` through to the snapshot.
   762→- Bumped `pilot-state.ts` `STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION` from 6 → 7 so the singleton rebuilds with the new `chf` field (otherwise the old cached state would have `s.chf = undefined`, producing `NaN` in CHF computations).

   838→### engine.ts (ADD only — no removals, no rewrites)
   839→- Imports: added `ADMISSIBILITY_ENVELOPES, BASE_FIXINGS` from `./blueprint`; `maseEnsemble, applyEnvelopes, smoothWeights, COMPONENTS, type PriceData, type VolatilityData, type MarketRegime, type WeightVector, type Component` from `./mase`; `marpDecision, type MarpDecision` from `./marp`. All existing imports RETAINED.
   840→- ReserveState: added `maseSmoothed: WeightVector | null` (EMA prior — null at genesis → STRATEGIC_PRIOR fallback) and `maseLastAt: number`.
   841→- initReserveState: initialises `maseSmoothed: null`, `maseLastAt: 0`.
   842→- MetricsSnapshot: added four new fields per the task spec — `mase: { models, ensembleTarget }`, `weightStates: { prior, target, smoothed, execution }`, `marp: { decisions, totalTradeUsd }`, `envelopes: { component, lower, upper, current, status }[]`.
   843→- computeSnapshot: after the existing logic, calls `buildMaseSnapshot(s, fx, vals, nav, rr)` (a pure helper) and merges the returned `mase`, `weightStates`, `marp`, `envelopes` into the snapshot. NO state mutation — the snapshot is idempotent across multiple computeSnapshot calls within a single tick.
   844→- New helper functions (all added; nothing rewritten):
   845→  - `estimateVolsFromState(s)` — pilot vol estimates: per-asset baseline × VIX/18.5 (30 VIX ≈ doubles vols, 12 VIX ≈ halves).
   846→  - `detectRegime(vix, dxy, goldVol)` — 0=calm, 1=normal, 2=stress, 3=crisis. VIX thresholds <15/<22/<30/≥30. DXY extremes (<88 or >115) bump regime to ≥2.
   847→  - `buildPriceData(fx)` — `P_{i,t} / P_{i,0}` for all 7 components using `BASE_FIXINGS`.
   848→  - `buildObservedWeights(vals, nav)` — converts `reserveAssetValues` into a `WeightVector` (each net value / NAV).
   849→  - `buildMaseSnapshot(s, fx, vals, nav, rr)` — the pure helper that runs the full pipeline: MASE ensemble → apply envelopes → EMA-smooth toward constrained target (uses s.maseSmoothed as prev) → build observed execution weights → MARP per-component decisions → envelope status per component (ok/warn/breach with 10% band edge as the warn threshold).
   850→  - `advanceMase(s, fx)` — exported; the ONLY function that mutates `s.maseSmoothed`. Called ONCE per tick from the pilot-state tick loop, and ONCE at cold start in `ensureStore`, so the EMA advances exactly once per 4s tick regardless of how many computeSnapshot calls happen in between.
   851→
   852→### Design decision: where to persist the EMA smoothed weights
   853→- The task said "In computeSnapshot(), after the existing calculations, add: ... Call smoothWeights(prevSmoothed, constrained) for the smoothed weights". This is satisfied — `buildMaseSnapshot` (called from `computeSnapshot`) computes smoothed weights using `s.maseSmoothed` as the prev.
   854→- However, mutating `s.maseSmoothed` directly inside `computeSnapshot` would cause the EMA to advance multiple times per tick (pilot-state's tick loop calls computeSnapshot 2× per tick + 1× per `/api/metrics` poll), converging the EMA faster than intended.
   855→- Solution: `advanceMase(s, fx)` is the only mutator (called once per tick from the tick loop + once at cold start). `buildMaseSnapshot` is a pure read-only helper that recomputes target/smoothed/MARP/envelopes using `s.maseSmoothed` as prev — so multiple computeSnapshot calls within a single tick return identical `mase`, `weightStates`, `marp`, `envelopes` values (idempotent).
   856→
   857→### pilot-state.ts
   858→- Bumped `STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION` 7 → 8 (forces the singleton to rebuild with the new `maseSmoothed` + `maseLastAt` fields).
   859→- Imported `advanceMase` from `./engine`.
   860→- Called `advanceMase(state, fx)` in `ensureStore` right after `initReserveState` (primes the EMA at genesis so the very first snapshot has a non-null prev-smoothed).
   861→- Called `advanceMase(store.state, store.fx)` in the tick loop after `advanceMacro` and before `updatePegHealth` (so lastVix/lastDxy are fresh, and the first computeSnapshot of the tick reads the freshly-persisted smoothed weights as the EMA prior).

  1061→- Created /home/z/my-project/contracts/MTQSigmaV2.sol (1057 lines, MIT, pragma ^0.8.20, single-file, no external imports). Implements ALL 12 required sections:
  1062→  1. ERC-20 + inline AccessControl (DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE / ADMIN_ROLE / MINTER_ROLE / PAUSER_ROLE / KEEPER_ROLE / ORACLE_ROLE) + Pausable (pause()/unpause(), whenNotPaused on mint/redeem/executeRebalance/commitFxRatesFromOracles). Constructor grants deployer all operational roles so the contract is immediately functional; MINTER_ROLE not granted (mint() is public).
  1063→  2. §2 7-Component GFB Index (chain-linked): PRIOR_USD/EUR/JPY/GBP/CNY/CHF/GOLD + BASE_*_USD fixings (all immutable constants), GFB_BASE_DENOMINATOR computed ONCE in constructor as immutable (chain-linked — NOT recomputed per call), getGFB() = Σ(W^Prior_i × P_i,t) / GFB_BASE_DENOMINATOR with CHF and Gold FIRST-CLASS. getMTQPrice() returns getGFB().
  1064→  3. §3 safety band (0.50e18 / 2.00e18), getMTQPriceWithGuard() reverts outside, getCirculatingSupply() = totalSupply − genesisReserveBalance, getLiability() = circulating × price / 1e18.
  1065→  4. §3.4.2 + §12 mint (USDC pull 6 dec, fee mintFeeBps default 10, netUsd18 × 1e18 / price, throttle by status: NORMAL 100% / CAUTION 50% / RECOVERY 25% / DEFENSIVE+EMERGENCY paused) and redeem (burn MTQ, grossUsd18 = mtq × price / 1e18, fee by status: NORMAL/CAUTION 15 bps / DEFENSIVE 50 bps / EMERGENCY 200 bps, net → USDC 6 dec, released from reserveVault).
  1066→  5. §14.1 enum Status { NORMAL, CAUTION, DEFENSIVE, EMERGENCY, RECOVERY }; setProtocolStatus(Status) is KEEPER_ROLE (MARP-keeper hook); emits StatusChanged.
  1067→  6. §8.1 WeightRegistry struct { uint256[7] targetWeights, smoothedWeights, executionWeights, uint256 lastUpdatedAt } in canonical order [USD, EUR, JPY, GBP, CNY, CHF, Gold]; enum Component { USD, EUR, JPY, GBP, CNY, CHF, Gold }; per-component admissibility envelopes as constant bounds (ENV_USD_LOWER/UPPER etc.); commitWeights(target, smoothed, execution) is ORACLE_ROLE/KEEPER_ROLE only, validates EVERY weight against its per-component envelope (reverts on breach), emits WeightsCommitted; getWeights() and getWeight(Component) view helpers; envelopeLower/envelopeUpper pure getters.
  1068→  7. §10 MARP: struct RebalanceTrade { Component component; int256 direction; uint256 tradeUsd; uint256 level; string reason; }; executeRebalance(RebalanceTrade[] calldata) is KEEPER_ROLE + whenNotPaused; for each trade validates level 1-6, enforces 24h direction lock (opposite direction blocked within 24h), validates trade moves toward the latest committed executionWeight (buy only if currentW < execW; sell only if currentW > execW), validates post-trade weight within 5pp absolute tolerance of execW, enforces daily turnover ≤ 5% of NAV (24h window auto-resets), updates reserveHeldUsd mirror, tracks lastDirection + lastRebalanceAt; emits RebalanceExecuted.
  1069→  8. §9 interface IOracleAdapter { getPrice(bytes32 pair) returns (price, timestamp, confidence) }; 3 adapter slots (chainlinkAdapter / pythAdapter / chronicleAdapter), setOracleAdapter(uint8 source, address) is ORACLE_ROLE (0=Chainlink, 1=Pyth, 2=Chronicle); pair identifiers via keccak256 (PAIR_EUR_USD, PAIR_GBP_USD, PAIR_JPY_USD, PAIR_CNY_USD, PAIR_CHF_USD, PAIR_XAU_USD); getOracleConsensus(bytes32 pair) public view implements §9.2 (staleness ≤60s, confidence <1%, deviation <2.5% from median, all via try/catch per adapter) and §9.3 (median(3) / average(2) / paused(<2)) returning (finalPrice, validCount, method, paused_, prices[3], valid[3]); commitFxRatesFromOracles() is KEEPER_ROLE + whenNotPaused — pulls consensus for all 6 pairs and stores as the live FX rates driving getGFB(); setFxRates() is the ORACLE_ROLE pilot fallback.
  1070→  9. §5 interface IAssetRegistry { getAsset(bytes32 currencyCode) returns (token, haircut, state, issuerId) }; setAssetRegistry(address) is ADMIN_ROLE; getReserveNetAssetValue() pulls haircuts from the registry (frozen/delisted assets contribute 0; try/catch fallback to no-haircut if the call fails or no registry is set); getReserveNavUsd() is the raw gross sum; getReserveRatio() = NAV / liability.
  1071→  10. §14.2 minimal timelock: struct ParameterChange { bytes32 key; uint256 newValue; uint256 queuedAt; uint256 executesAt; bool executed; }; mapping(bytes32 => ParameterChange) paramChanges; queueChange(key, newValue) is ADMIN_ROLE and sets executesAt = block.timestamp + 48h (TIMELOCK_DELAY); executeChange(key) is callable by ANYONE after the timelock elapses (the timelock is the protection, not the caller restriction) — applies via _applyParam which validates + sets mintFeeBps / redeemFeeBps / reserveRatioTarget; cancelChange(key) is ADMIN_ROLE pre-execution; parameter keys PARAM_MINT_FEE_BPS / PARAM_REDEEM_FEE_BPS / PARAM_RESERVE_RATIO_TARGET all keccak256-hashed; emits ParameterQueued / ParameterExecuted / ParameterCancelled.
  1072→  11. §25 getHonestStatus() external pure returns (implementedMask = 0x7FF, blueprintMajor = 1, contractVersion = 1, statusDeclaration = the v1.0 master-declaration string). Bit encoding documented inline identically to the v1.2 pilot's getHonestStatus — same 11 bits, same positions — so V2's 0x7FF is verifiably the "all-on complement" of the pilot's 0x400.
  1073→  12. Genesis + reserve accounting: genesisMint(amount) is ADMIN_ROLE one-shot (genesisDone guard), mints to genesisReserve (default address(this), settable via setGenesisReserve), updates genesisReserveBalance; setReserveVault(v), bootstrapReserveHoldings(uint256[7]) and setReserveHolding(Component, uint256) for the on-chain reserve mirror.
  1074→  13. Events: full set — Transfer, Approval, Mint, Redeem, PriceUpdated, StatusChanged, WeightsCommitted, RebalanceExecuted, OracleAdapterSet, AssetRegistrySet, ParameterQueued, ParameterExecuted, ParameterCancelled, RoleGranted, RoleRevoked, Paused, Unpaused (17 events total per ABI).

  1104→- Created `src/lib/mtq/audit-trail.ts` (~237 lines) with 4 async helpers:
  1105→  * `persistDailyStateVector(snapshot, tickCount, lastPersistedAt)` — §24.1, throttled 30s. Writes one DailyStateVector row carrying the full 28-field monetary snapshot (GFB Index, MTQ price, NAV, liability, RR, LCR, status, supply, 7-component reserve net values, VIX/DXY + z-scores + rawTheta + smoothed gold weight, target/observed gold weights, buffer state + ratio, eject stage, pegHealth JSON).
  1106→  * `persistRebalancingDecision(tickCount, decision, navUsd, reserveRatio, observedGoldWeight, targetGoldWeight, deviationPct, applied, postTrade?)` — §24.2, every evaluated rebalance. Records the decision (should/shouldn't, direction, tradeUsd, reason, blockedBy) AND when applied, the pre/post-trade gold/fiat net, post-trade RR, post-trade observed gold weight.
  1107→  * `persistOracleSamples(oracle, tickCount, lastPersistedAt)` — §24.3, throttled 30s, batched in a single `db.$transaction`. Writes 5 rows per tick (one per pair EUR/USD, GBP/USD, JPY/USD, CNY/USD, XAU/USD) capturing the 3 feeds' validity + price, validCount, finalPrice, method (median/average/paused), spreadBps, paused, and a JSON of discard reasons.
  1108→  * `persistMarpDecisions(tickCount, snapshot)` — §10 + §24.2. Writes one RebalancingDecision row per MARP component (6 rows per tick) so the per-component MARP decisions are also auditable. Encodes `direction` as +1/-1/0 (buy/sell/hold), `reason` as `[MARP:{component}] {reason}`, `applied=false` (MARP decisions are advisory in the pilot — not executed as separate trades).
  1109→  * Helper `maxEjectStage(snapshot)` — collapses the per-currency `ejectStage` map (the schema column is a single Int) to the MAX stage across currencies as the worst-case severity indicator.
  1110→  * Helper `fin(v)` — coerces non-finite numbers (Infinity/NaN) to 0 for SQLite Float storage. Used for `reserveRatio` / `lcr` / `postReserveRatio` which are `Infinity` at genesis (no circulating supply → liability = 0 → RR = NAV/0). Documented convention: `reserveRatio = 0` in the audit log means "N/A — no circulating supply"; once the first mint occurs, circulating supply > 0 and the real finite ratio is persisted.

  1137→Issues encountered & fixed during this task:
  1138→- (A) Prisma client stale in `globalThis` cache: the running dev server had cached a `PrismaClient` instance from BEFORE the orchestrator added the 3 audit-trail models, so `db.dailyStateVector` / `db.rebalancingDecision` / `db.oracleSample` were all `undefined` on the cached client → `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'create')`. The orchestrator's `bun run db:push` regenerated the client in `node_modules/.prisma/client/`, but the running dev server had the old instance pinned in `globalThis.prisma` (the `db.ts` cache). HMR does not invalidate the globalThis Prisma cache (and Turbopack does not HMR `node_modules`). Fix: ran `bun run db:generate` to regenerate, then restarted the dev server (the system kills manually-launched `bun run dev` processes, so used `setsid -f bash -c 'exec bun run dev >> dev.log 2>&1'` to fully detach; the restarted server loaded the new Prisma client from `node_modules/.prisma/client/`). After restart, `db.dailyStateVector` / `db.rebalancingDecision` / `db.oracleSample` are all defined and persistence works.
  1139→- (B) `reserveRatio = Infinity` at genesis stripped by Prisma: in the genesis state (no mints yet), `circulatingSupply = 0` → `liability = 0` → `computeReserveRatio()` returns `Infinity`. SQLite cannot store Infinity, and Prisma's Float validator strips `Infinity` for non-nullable Float columns, treating the field as "missing" → `PrismaClientValidationError: Argument 'reserveRatio' is missing`. (Same root cause for `lcr` and the post-trade `postReserveRatio`.) Fix: added the `fin()` helper in `audit-trail.ts` that coerces non-finite numbers to 0, with a clear documented convention in the file header (`reserveRatio = 0` in the audit log = "N/A — no circulating supply"). The existing `/api/simulate/mint/route.ts` and `/api/simulate/redeem/route.ts` `pilotTrial.create` calls have the same latent issue but never hit it in practice (they're only called after a mint, when circulating supply > 0). Did NOT modify those routes (out of scope: only audit-trail.ts and pilot-state.ts are owned by this task).
  1140→- (C) HMR didn't invalidate the singleton's setInterval tick closure after the `fin()` fix: the singleton was rebuilt (STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION 8→9) BEFORE the `fin()` fix was added, so the singleton's tick closure pinned the OLD `audit-trail.ts` (the version with the Infinity bug). Editing `audit-trail.ts` triggered Turbopack to recompile it, but the singleton's `setInterval` callback closure still referenced the OLD module exports → the `fin()` fix didn't take effect until the dev server was restarted. Fix: killed the next dev process and restarted with the same `setsid -f` pattern. After restart, the singleton rebuilt from the LATEST code and `fin()` is now applied.

  1161→### Part 1 — §14.1 Constitutional Separation of Gold (engine.ts, ADD-only)
  1162→- ReserveState: added 4 new fields `indexPaxg` / `indexXaut` / `reservePaxg` / `reserveXaut` (gold locked to back the index weight vs gold in the reserve buffer that MARP rebalances). Legacy `paxg` / `xaut` retained as totals (= index + reserve) so existing readers (concentration optimizer, applyRedeem) work unchanged. Added `rebalancePath: 'legacy' | 'marp'` to track the active execution path.
  1163→- initReserveState: split the genesis gold 50/50 between index and reserve (documented choice — 50/50 chosen over the 26%/74% alternative because at genesis the total gold IS exactly 26% of NAV, so a 26/74 split would leave index gold at only ~6.8% of NAV, far below the 26% index weight it's supposed to back). Set `rebalancePath: 'legacy'` default.
  1164→- Added `commitIndexGold(s, indexPaxg, indexXaut)` — keeper-role equivalent. Sets the locked index gold, re-derives reserve = max(0, total − index), defensive against over-commit (bumps total + logs warn), emits `IndexGoldCommitted` event-equivalent via console.log.
  1165→- Added `syncReserveFromTotal(s)` and `syncTotalFromReserve(s)` — the only sanctioned ways to maintain the §14.1 invariant. The former is called after the legacy path / concentration optimizer mutates `s.paxg` / `s.xaut` directly; the latter after the MARP path mutates `s.reservePaxg` / `s.reserveXaut`.
  1166→- reserveAssetValues: added 6 new return fields — `indexPaxgUsd`, `indexXautUsd`, `reservePaxgUsd`, `reserveXautUsd`, `indexGoldNet`, `reserveGoldNet` (USD net of haircut). Defensive fallback to `max(0, s.paxg - s.indexPaxg)` if `s.reservePaxg < 0`.
  1167→- MetricsSnapshot: added `reserve.indexGoldNet` + `reserve.reserveGoldNet` (the §14.1 gold split), `rebalancePath: 'legacy' | 'marp'` (which path is mutating state), and `marpExecution: { appliedCount, skippedCount, totalTradeUsd, path } | null` (read-only projection of what MARP WOULD do this tick — computed in buildMaseSnapshot, no state mutation).
  1168→- buildObservedWeights: added a clear §14.1 comment — the Gold component's observed weight uses the TOTAL gold (= index + reserve, USD-net). Both pools back the 26% Strategic Prior Gold weight; only the reserve is MARP-rebalanced. Index gold is locked and only changes via `commitIndexGold()`.
  1169→- buildMaseSnapshot: added `marpExecution` computation — counts decisions with `shouldTrade && level >= 6` as `appliedCount` (would-execute), the rest as `skippedCount`, sums their `tradeUsd` as `totalTradeUsd`. `path` mirrors `s.rebalancePath`.
  1170→- applyRebalanceTrade (legacy §7 path): added 2 lines that mirror the gold delta to `s.reservePaxg` (the buffer, NOT index gold). Total `s.paxg` change unchanged; reserve split tracks it.
  1171→- applyRedeem (§12 redemption): added 1 line — `syncReserveFromTotal(s)` after the gold release.
  1172→- rebalanceForConcentration (§5.6 multi-issuer optimizer): added 1 line — `syncReserveFromTotal(s)` after the PAXG/XAUT re-split.
  1173→
  1174→### Part 2 — Wire MARP into the rebalance execution path
  1175→- engine.ts: added `applyMarpRebalance(s, fx, decisions: MarpExecDecision[])` — the v1.0 production-target rebalance path. Takes the per-component decisions (accepts both the full `MarpDecision[]` from `marp.ts` and the trimmed version from `snapshot.marp.decisions`). For each decision with `shouldTrade === true` and `level >= 6`: enforces `MAX_DAILY_TURNOVER` (5% NAV) and `DIRECTION_LOCK_HOURS` (24h whipsaw guard); applies the trade to the RESERVE holdings (index gold is LOCKED — never touched). Trade execution model: non-USD fiat components pair with USD (split 1/3 across USDC/USDP/USDT); USD pairs with Gold (split 50/50 across `reservePaxg + reserveXaut`); Gold pairs with USD. Re-syncs the gold totals via `syncTotalFromReserve(s)` after each gold-affecting trade. Updates `s.lastTradeDir`, `s.lastTradeAt`, `s.dailyTurnoverUsd`. Returns `{ appliedCount, skippedCount, totalTradeUsd, path: 'marp', traces }` where `traces` is a per-decision trace (component, direction, tradeUsd, level, applied, skipReason?).
  1176→- pilot-state.ts: added `const USE_MARP_EXECUTION = false` (default — keeps legacy §7 for pilot stability; MARP is the v1.0 production target). Bumped `STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION` 9 → 10 (P0-B bumped 8 → 9; I bump 9 → 10 per task spec so the singleton auto-rebuilds with both sets of new fields). In `tick()`: set `store.state.rebalancePath` each tick (idempotent), added an `if (USE_MARP_EXECUTION) { ... } else if (...) { ... }` chain. The `if` branch is my new MARP path (guarded by `!store.oracle.anyPaused && snap0.priceInBand`, wrapped in try/catch, logs only when trades applied). The `else if` branch is P0-B's existing legacy block (body unchanged — only `if` → `else if`). I did NOT modify P0-B's `let rebalanceAudit` block or the audit-trail persistence calls after `store.tickCount += 1`. When USE_MARP_EXECUTION=true, `rebalanceAudit` stays null and P0-B's `if (rebalanceAudit)` skips cleanly; MARP decisions are still captured by P0-B's `persistMarpDecisions` call (reads `snapshot.marp.decisions`), so audit-trail coverage is preserved for both paths.

  1217→Stage Summary:
  1218→- §14.1 Constitutional Separation of Gold is now implemented in the TS engine: index gold (PAXG + XAUT) is locked in a separate accounting vault that backs the 26% Strategic Prior Gold weight; reserve gold (the other half at genesis) is the buffer MARP rebalances. `commitIndexGold()` (keeper-role equivalent) is the only way to move gold between the two pools. The §14.1 invariant (`paxg = indexPaxg + reservePaxg`, same for xaut) is maintained via `syncReserveFromTotal()` and `syncTotalFromReserve()` helpers called from `applyRebalanceTrade`, `applyRedeem`, `rebalanceForConcentration`, and `applyMarpRebalance`. The gold split is exposed in the snapshot (`reserve.indexGoldNet` + `reserve.reserveGoldNet`) and rendered in the new MARP tab.
  1219→- MARP per-component rebalance execution is now wired into the tick loop via a feature flag (`USE_MARP_EXECUTION = false` by default — keeps legacy §7 for pilot stability; MARP is the v1.0 production target). The new `applyMarpRebalance()` function takes the per-component `MarpDecision[]` and applies each "would-execute" trade (level >= 6) to the RESERVE holdings (index gold locked), enforcing `MAX_DAILY_TURNOVER` (5% NAV) and `DIRECTION_LOCK_HOURS` (24h whipsaw guard). The trade-execution model pairs each non-USD component with USD (split 1/3 across USDC/USDP/USDT) and USD with Gold (split 50/50 PAXG/XAUT in the reserve buffer), so every trade has a clear counterparty and the total NAV is preserved (modulo haircut differences).
  1220→- The RebalanceEngine.tsx UI now renders both paths side-by-side via a tab toggle: the Legacy §7 view (single-direction, observed vs target gold weight, deviation, trade USD, λ1-λ4, execution constraints) and the new MARP view (per-component table with direction/tradeUsd/urgency/level/reason, §14.1 gold split cards, summary "X applied · Y skipped · $Z total · path = legacy/marp", 6-level hierarchy legend, footer note about index gold being locked). An A/B badge at the top shows "Legacy §7 active" / "MARP active" based on `snapshot.rebalancePath`. An honest note explains the v1.0 production target / pilot fallback relationship and points the user at the feature flag in `pilot-state.ts`.

  1352→Task ID: P1-A

  1354→Task: Backend AI gateway — Gemini + Groq + Hugging Face integration (4 lib modules + 4 API routes, server-side only, with graceful fallback rules)

  1356→Work Log:
  1357→- Read worklog (1270 lines) to understand the MTQΣ pilot architecture: in-process engine singleton (4s tick), `MetricsSnapshot` shape, `getSnapshot()` from `@/lib/mtq/pilot-state`, existing `/api/*` route pattern (NextResponse.json + try/catch).
  1358→- Read `src/lib/mtq/engine.ts` lines 1075-1154 (MetricsSnapshot interface) and `src/lib/mtq/registry.ts` (ConcentrationReport shape) to discover the actual engine contract: `ejectStage` is `{ USD, EUR, GBP, JPY, CNY }` (per-currency 0-4), `concentration[].sharePct` is a 0-1 fraction (not a percent), and `RR / LCR` are `Infinity` when `liability = 0` (no MTQ minted yet).
  1359→- Created 9 new files (only in my ownership):
  1360→  * `src/lib/ai/keys.ts` — server-side key validation. Reads `process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY / GROQ_API_KEY / HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY` ONCE at module load. Exports `getKeys()` (throws if `typeof window !== 'undefined'` — defense in depth), `hasKey(provider)`, and 4 feature flags `AI_BRIEFING_ENABLED / AI_QA_ENABLED / AI_RISK_SIGNALS_ENABLED / AI_SCREEN_ENABLED` (default true; truthy "true"/"1"/"on", falsy "false"/"0"/"off").
  1361→  * `src/lib/ai/policy-briefer.ts` — `generateBriefing(snapshot)` returns `{ briefing, generatedAt, model, sources, disclaimer }`. Builds a 3-paragraph COO-voice prompt (State / Risk Watch / Recommended Governance Actions), POSTs to Gemini `v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`, extracts text from `candidates[0].content.parts[].text`. 60s in-memory cache (module-level `let cached`). Graceful fallback: deterministic rule-based briefing derived from the snapshot with `model = "fallback-rules"` and a disclaimer mutation. 10s `AbortSignal.timeout`.
  1362→  * `src/lib/ai/blueprint-qa.ts` — `answerQuestion(question)` returns `{ answer, citations, model, disclaimer }`. Sanitizes input (max 500 chars, strip control chars, reject empty). Builds a long-context prompt by serializing the authoritative TS constants from `src/lib/mtq/blueprint.ts` (STRATEGIC_PRIOR, ADMISSIBILITY_ENVELOPES, RISK_STATE_MACHINE, GOVERNANCE_HIERARCHY, HONEST_STATUS, etc.) — NOT the raw 3270-line docx. Uses `systemInstruction` for the doc-assistant role. Extracts §-number citations via regex `/§\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+){0,2})\b/g`. 12s `AbortSignal.timeout`. Graceful fallback: spec-mandated "Blueprint Q&A unavailable. The full v1.0 blueprint is documented in the Docs section above." with `model = "fallback"`.
  1363→  * `src/lib/ai/risk-signals.ts` — `generateRiskSignals(snapshot)` returns `{ signals, generatedAt, model }`. POSTs to Groq `openai/v1/chat/completions` with `model: "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"`, `temperature: 0.3`, `max_tokens: 800`. Parses the model's JSON array, strips markdown code fences, validates each signal (severity in `critical|warning|info`, title ≤ 80 chars, detail ≤ 300 chars). 15s in-memory cache. Graceful fallback: rule-based signals (oracle paused / concentration breach / concentration warn / RR < 1.05 / eject active / status degraded / else "All systems normal"). 8s `AbortSignal.timeout`.
  1364→  * `src/lib/ai/sanctions-screen.ts` — `screenText(text)` returns `{ entities, model, disclaimer }`. POSTs to HF Inference API for `dbmdz/bert-large-cased-finetuned-conll03-english`. Filters score ≥ 0.85, dedupes by surface word (case-insensitive), maps `entity_group` (PER/ORG/LOC/MISC) to readable types. 15s `AbortSignal.timeout`. Graceful fallback: regex finds capitalized multi-word phrases (poor-man's NER, clearly labelled `"Unknown (regex)"`); skips common sentence-starter single words. Max 5000 chars.
  1365→  * `src/app/api/ai/briefing/route.ts` — GET, `force-dynamic`, `revalidate = 0`. If `AI_BRIEFING_ENABLED=false` → HTTP 503 `{ error, model: "disabled" }`. Else: `getSnapshot()` → `generateBriefing()` → JSON. Try/catch → HTTP 500 `{ error, model: "error" }`.
  1366→  * `src/app/api/ai/qa/route.ts` — POST, same pattern. Reads `{ question }` from body (tolerates empty/invalid JSON).

  1454→  2. **RR decay under blueprint-intended design** (fees → Operational Wallet, excluded from NAV): RR goes from ∞ (genesis, L=0) → 2.10 at $1M minted → 1.10 at $11M → 1.04 at $25M (STRESS) → 1.011 at $110M → 1.0022 at $500M → 1.0011 at $1B minted.   1456→- Identified 3 CRITICAL blueprint-vs-implementation mismatches:
  1457→  • **F-FEE-01**: MTQSigmaV2.sol lines 473 + 516 route all USDC (incl. fees) to reserveVault, contradicting §20.4 (fees → Operational Wallet, excluded from NAV). Inflates reported RR by ~10–25 bps, breaks the §20.2 three-pool accounting separation, leaves no operational wallet for gas/oracle/keeper costs.
  1458→  • **F-EMERG-01**: The user's checklist premise ("§21 says redemption is still allowed (with increased fee)") is INCORRECT — it describes the v1.2 pilot's behavior, not the v1.0 blueprint. The blueprint §21.4 state matrix explicitly shows "Redemption → Paused" in S5 EMERGENCY. The contract's 5-state machine only raises the fee to 2% in EMERGENCY; it does NOT pause. This is the most important divergence in the entire tokenomics model — it allows a death spiral scenario under combined USDC depeg + gold crash + oracle failure.
  1459→  • **F-RISK-01**: The contract implements a 5-state machine (NORMAL/CAUTION/DEFENSIVE/EMERGENCY/RECOVERY) with thresholds 1.10/1.05/1.00. The blueprint §21.2 specifies a 6-state machine (NORMAL/CAUTION/STRESS/DEFENSIVE/EMERGENCY/RECOVERY) with thresholds 1.10/1.05/1.02/1.00 and fee schedule 0.15/0.50/1.00/paused/0.30. The contract collapses STRESS+DEFENSIVE into one "DEFENSIVE" at 0.50%.

  1504→  * §9.2/§9.3 chain-linked index (lines 4060–4140, especially line 4090 `updateIndex()` and line 3972 `chainLinkDivisor` "cumulative product of D_t"): the blueprint's recursive `I_t = I_{t-1} × Σ W_{i,t-1}·(P_{i,t}/P_{i,t-1})` form. Contract has only an immutable `GFB_BASE_DENOMINATOR` (line 182, set once in constructor at lines 364–372) and a fresh-eval `getGFB()` (lines 420–431) — a fixed-base Laspeyres index, not a chain-linked recursive index → CRITICAL finding C2, bit-3 overstated.
  1505→  * §10.3 MARP 6-level hierarchy (lines 4507–4616): Levels 0–5 = Monitor / Natural Flow / Drift Trigger / Risk Trigger / Structural Change / Emergency. Contract's RebalanceTrade.level (line 256) is encoded as 1–6 (off-by-one) and is a free keeper-supplied input — the contract does NOT enforce the hierarchy gates → MEDIUM finding M2, contributes to bit-6 being overstated.
  1506→  * §20.4 fee accrual (line 13338: "Fees accrue to the Operational Wallet") and line 14178 ("assets in the Operational Wallet and Cold Treasury are not counted toward the Reserve Ratio"). Contract commingles fees with collateral in a single reserveVault (line 473) → LOW finding L5.
  1507→- Ran the audit checklist (14 sections, ~80 sub-items) covering ERC-20 correctness, inline AccessControl, Pausable, reentrancy (CEI per function), the IOracleAdapter try/catch + §9.2 staleness/confidence/deviation + §9.3 median(3)/average(2)/paused(<2) consensus, the MASE Weight Registry (commitWeights + admissibility envelopes + per-component consistency + sum-to-1 invariant), MARP executeRebalance (24h direction lock, daily turnover cap, 5pp tolerance, RR<1.05 override), mint/redeem (decimal scaling 1e12 both directions, status throttle, fee schedule), genesis + reserve (one-shot, collateral backing, vault zero-check, bootstrap overwrite), DAO timelock (queueChange/executeChange/cancelChange, parameter keys), getHonestStatus bit-by-bit, tokenomics (CFO view), gas/DoS, and upgradeability.

  1585→### Implementation — `src/lib/mtq/audit-stress.ts` (NEW, 1,290 lines)
  1586→- Self-contained stress-test runner that imports ONLY the engine's public surface (no edits to engine.ts, blueprint.ts, etc.).
  1587→- Public exports: `StressResult`, `StressTestSuite`, `runAllStressTests(): Promise<StressTestSuite>`.
  1588→- Internal helpers: `mulberry32` (deterministic PRNG), `gaussian` (Box-Muller), `cauchy` (heavy-tailed), `makeFx` (build FxSnapshot), `stepEngine` (lightweight per-tick evaluator — reserveAssetValues + ratios + rebalance, no MASE/MARP), `setupInitialState` (init + 9M USD mint + redistributeToStrategicPrior), `redistributeToStrategicPrior` (re-splits holdings to 7-component Strategic Prior composition so gold = 26% of NAV from the start), `aggregateRuns` (TrajectoryResult[] → StressResult), `buildSyntheticHistory` + `historyToReturns` + block-bootstrap, `runTrajectory` (one MC trajectory over N ticks with an FX generator), `runPerturbedSim` (self-contained engine sim with perturbed α/β/θ_max/λ/λ1..λ4 — mirrors engine.ts formulas but accepts parameter overrides; needed because the blueprint constants are immutable `const` exports and can't be monkey-patched in a TypeScript-safe way).
  1589→- Critical engine-wiring note for MC sims: each tick represents 1 day of simulated time. The engine's `maybeResetDailyTurnover` uses wall-clock time (Date.now()), which doesn't advance inside a tight MC loop — so `stepEngine` resets `s.dailyTurnoverUsd = 0`, `s.lastTurnoverResetAt = Date.now()`, `s.lastTradeAt = 0` at the start of each tick. This is the standard "1 tick = 1 day" interpretation for MC stress sims and prevents the rebalancer from being permanently throttled by the wall-clock turnover cap.
  1590→- Critical initial-state note: after `applyMint(9M USD)`, the gold weight of NAV drops to ~2.8% (genesis had 26% gold but the $9M user mint adds only USD). The engine's rebalancer would restore gold to ~26% over ~7 days, but in a tight MC loop the wall-clock turnover cap never resets. `setupInitialState` therefore calls `redistributeToStrategicPrior(s, fx)` after the initial mint — preserves total NAV + total supply but re-splits the holdings across all 7 Strategic Prior components so gold = 26% from the start. This faithfully matches the engine's design intent (Strategic Prior is the long-term anchor).
  1591→

  1617→
  1618→### Top 3 critical findings (in the report)
  1619→1. CRITICAL — Structural short-gold exposure: the engine's `computeGfbIndex` uses UNNORMALISED spot prices (Laspeyres price index), so gold at $2,500/oz contributes 99.9% of the GFB index by USD notional — despite the Strategic Prior reserving only 26% gold. A +50% gold shock crashes RR from 1.10 → 0.83 in 100% of S5 runs. The blueprint's stated intent is the chain-linked formula `Σ W_i × (P_{i,t}/P_{i,0})` (which would give gold 26% of index RISK, matching the reserve), but the engine's implementation creates the mismatch. This single architectural issue dominates 6 of 11 scenarios (S1, S2, S3, S4, S5, S11).
  1620→2. HIGH — Cauchy / fat-tailed distributions decimate survival (S3: 35.1% survival, worst RR 0.146). Tail events amplify the structural short-gold exposure into RR wipeouts. Only scenario where MTQ price exits the [0.50, 2.00] safety band (61% peg stability).
  1621→3. MEDIUM — Scenario design gaps: S9 (redemption run from RR=1.10 is accretive, so no fee escalation ever triggers) and S10 (combined shock with Gold -20% is benign, not stressful, because Gold -20% IMPROVES RR by the structural-short-gold math). Both §23.10 and §23.11 test specifications should be amended to start from a stress-position initial state and/or include gold-appreciation shocks.
  1622→

  1624→Breakdown: Survival 35/50 (6/11 fully pass; 4 partial; 1 catastrophic) + Robustness 12/20 (low parameter sensitivity but absolute survival well below target) + Recovery 12/15 (5/6 stressed scenarios recover; S5 never recovers) + Peg stability 14/15 (96.5% average; only Cauchy tails push price out of band).

  1700→- §0 Executive summary with the one-paragraph reconciliation proof (COO-17): the chain-linked recursion I_t = I_{t-1}·Σ W_{i,t-1}·(P_{i,t}/P_{i,t-1}) is exact by construction; the divisor D_t = B_t^-/B_t^+ absorbs the compositional change so the published level I_t^+ = G_t·B_t^+ = G_{t-1}·B_t^- = I_t^- is unchanged (neutrality); the aggregate form I_t = G_t·Σ W_{i,t}·(P_{i,t}/P_{i,0}) reconstructs the recursion-published level exactly.

  1704→- §4 Aggregate↔recursive reconciliation (COO-17): the induction proof that I_t = G_t·Σ W_{i,t}·(P_{i,t}/P_{i,0}) for all t, with the base-relative pinned convention, and the exact reconciliation test |getIndex() − (chainLinkDivisor·getNavForm())/1e18| ≤ 1 wei.

  1706→- §6 Off-chain/on-chain equivalence: documented that the current engine.ts and MTQSigmaV2.sol do NOT implement the canonical form (they use Laspeyres with frozen strategic-prior weights and G_t=1) — this is the structural short-gold defect the audit caught.   1730→- The closure report pins the canonical index form: I_t = I_{t-1}·Σ W_{i,t-1}·(P_{i,t}/P_{i,t-1}) (COO-16), with the divisor D_t = B_t^-/B_t^+ absorbing weight changes (§9.3 neutrality), and the cumulative G_t = Π D_s carrying the entire chain-link history for the COO-17 aggregate-form reconstruction I_t = G_t·Σ W_{i,t}·(P_{i,t}/P_{i,0}).

This is the structural-symmetry argument: under the canonical form, the index moves by the gold WEIGHT (26%) times the gold price move (50%) = 13%, matching the reserve's 13% NAV move, so the ratio is unchanged.
  1732→- The index/NAV divergence δ_t = RR_t − 1 by construction — the divergence equals the over-collateralisation buffer. The state machine is the divergence's circuit breaker.
  1733→- The canonical state function is a pure deterministic function of (rr, lcr, previousState, now) — no human subjectivity. The worse-applicable-condition rule (max by restrictiveness rank) ensures the protocol never flatters itself by reading the better metric. Deterioration is immediate; recovery requires 48h sustained at EACH step (no skipping). EMERGENCY entry is immediate on RR<1.00 (Invariant I2 hard floor).

  1819→### P0-FIX-1 — Canonical chain-linked index (src/lib/mtq/chain-index.ts NEW, 252 lines)
  1820→- Created the canonical chain-linked index per Master Listing 3 / §9.2 COO-16. Formula: `I_t = I_{t-1} × Σ_i [W_{i,t-1} × (P_{i,t} / P_{i,t-1})]`.
  1821→- The 7-component canonical order is [USD, EUR, JPY, GBP, CNY, CHF, Gold].
  1822→- `initChainIndex(baseDenominator, strategicPrior, baseFixings, now)`: I_0 = 1.0, G_0 = 1.0, prevWeights = STRATEGIC_PRIOR, prevPrices = BASE_FIXINGS. Validates that strategicPrior sums to ~1.0 and that the array lengths are 7.
  1823→- `advanceIndex(state, currentPrices)`: computes periodReturn = Σ W_{i,t-1} × (P_{i,t}/P_{i,t-1}), I_t = I_{t-1} × periodReturn, updates prevPrices. Pure function (returns new state, does not mutate input).
  1824→- `commitWeights(state, newWeights, currentPrices, now)`: computes B_t^- = Σ W_{i,t-1} × P_{i,t} (old weights), B_t^+ = Σ W_{i,t} × P_{i,t} (new weights), D_t = B_t^- / B_t^+, G_t = G_{t-1} × D_t, I_t stays the SAME (zero-artificial-return). Updates prevWeights + prevPrices.
  1825→- `getMTQPrice(state)`: returns I_t × PAR (PAR = 1.0). The /INDEX_BASE_DENOMINATOR form in the spec comment would give P_MTQ ≈ 1/650 at genesis (wrong); the correct form for I_0 = 1.0 is P_MTQ = I_t × PAR.
  1826→- Convenience helpers: `fxToPriceVector(fx)`, `strategicPriorToWeights(prior)`, `baseFixingsToPrices(fixings)` — convert the engine's record shapes into the 7-component arrays.
  1827→- Inline self-test (`runSelfTest`): proves I_t = 1.13 after gold +50%, unchanged (1.13) after committing same weights AND after committing DIFFERENT weights (the zero-artificial-return property). The self-test runs at module load and logs a PASS/FAIL to console.
  1828→
  1829→### P0-FIX-2 — NAV-based redemption (engine.ts applyRedeem modified)
  1830→- OLD: `grossUsd = mtqAmount × price` (price = P_MTQ from the legacy GFB index).
  1831→- NEW: `NAV_t = V_net / circulatingSupply; grossUsd = mtqAmount × NAV_t`.
  1832→- Genesis fallback: at circulatingSupply = 0, NAV_t = P_MTQ (avoids division by zero). Documented in a multi-line comment explaining the post-first-mint behaviour (NAV_per_MTQ = $42/MTQ at RR=2400% is correct — the redeemer receives the actual book value).
  1833→- Fee uses the canonical `redeemFee(state)` from state-machine.ts (NORMAL/CAUTION 0.15%, STRESS 0.50%, DEFENSIVE 1.00%, EMERGENCY 2.00%, RECOVERY 0.50%). The legacy feeBps field is retained and computed from the canonical fraction × 10000.
  1834→- Redemption is PAUSED in EMERGENCY per §21.4 (the reconciliation resolves the §16.2 vs §21.4 contradiction in favour of §21.4: pause). Implemented via `redemptionAllowed(status)` from state-machine.ts.
  1835→- Audit fields restructured: the legacy `auditGrossUsdNav` (§12.2) is now the CANONICAL settlement value (= grossUsd); the legacy `grossUsd` (§3.4.2 index-priced) is now `auditGrossUsdIndex` (audit-only alternative). `auditDeltaUsd` = §12.2 − §3.4.2 (positive when NAV pays more, which is the expected case at RR > 100%).
  1836→- New field `navPerMtq` added to RedeemResult for transparency.
  1837→- Index/NAV divergence analysis (§12): added to MetricsSnapshot as `indexNavDivergence: { navPerMtq, pMtq, divergence, divergencePct, threshold, status }`. status = "normal" (<5%), "monitor" (5–10%), "stress" (>10%). At genesis (no circulating supply), divergence = 0 (NAV = P_MTQ fallback). After the first mint, divergence becomes large (1125% in the live pilot at RR=1.10 with $1.2M NAV behind 99,902 MTQ = $12.26/MTQ vs $1.00 index) — this is the EXPECTED behaviour the divergence analysis is designed to surface.
  1838→
  1839→### P0-FIX-3 — Canonical 6-state risk machine (src/lib/mtq/state-machine.ts NEW, 232 lines)
  1840→- Created the canonical 6-state machine: NORMAL, CAUTION, STRESS, DEFENSIVE, EMERGENCY, RECOVERY.
  1841→- ONE authoritative `determineState(rr, lcr, previousState, enteredAt, now, recoveryConfirmationPeriodMs)` function consumed by ALL modules. No module may independently implement its own divergent classification logic.
  1842→- "Worse condition binds" rule: takes the more severe of (rr-derived-state, lcr-derived-state). LCR = Infinity (no circulating supply → no stress demand) maps to NORMAL.
  1843→- Hysteresis: entry into EMERGENCY is immediate; entry into RECOVERY (from EMERGENCY/DEFENSIVE) requires RR ≥ 1.10 AND LCR ≥ 1.00 sustained for 48h (confirmationPeriodEnds = enteredAt + 48h); exit from RECOVERY to NORMAL requires the 48h to complete. During RECOVERY, if conditions worsen to DEFENSIVE/EMERGENCY, exit RECOVERY immediately (no hysteresis on getting worse). During RECOVERY, if conditions are CAUTION/STRESS (intermediate), STAY in RECOVERY (we're still in the 48h window; timer doesn't reset).
  1844→- `mintThrottle(state)`: NORMAL 1.0, CAUTION 0.5, STRESS 0 (paused), DEFENSIVE 0, EMERGENCY 0, RECOVERY 0.25.
  1845→- `redeemFee(state)`: NORMAL/CAUTION 0.0015, STRESS 0.005, DEFENSIVE 0.01, EMERGENCY 0.02, RECOVERY 0.005.
  1846→- `rebalanceUrgency(state)`: NORMAL 0.3, CAUTION 0.5, STRESS 0.8, DEFENSIVE 1.0, EMERGENCY 1.0, RECOVERY 0.6.
  1847→- `mintingAllowed(state)`: false for STRESS, DEFENSIVE, EMERGENCY.
  1848→- `redemptionAllowed(state)`: false for EMERGENCY (per §21.4 — NOT §16.2).
  1849→- `stressLevel(state)`: 0-4 integer for mase.ts's stress-adaptive smoothing (RECOVERY → 1, CAUTION-speed).
  1850→- Inline self-test (`runSelfTest`): 11 cases including all 6 state transitions, the worse-condition-binds rule, the RECOVERY 48h hysteresis (enter at 0ms, stay at 47h, exit at 49h, exit immediately on EMERGENCY), and the policy helpers. Runs at module load.
  1851→
  1852→### P0-FIX-4 — 4 governance layers (blueprint.ts additions)
  1853→- Added `GOVERNANCE_LAYERS` constant: CONSTITUTIONAL (7/7 Multi-Sig, 90 days), MONETARY (DAO 51%, 48h), RISK (Risk Council 4/7, 24h), EMERGENCY (Emergency Council 4/7, instant).
  1854→- Added `PARAMETER_REGISTRY` constant: 15 entries mapping each parameter to its owning layer. Constitutional parameters (PAR, RR_HARD_FLOOR, ADMISSIBILITY_ENVELOPES) are immutable. Monetary parameters (RR_TARGET, MINT_FEE_BPS, REDEEM_FEE_NORMAL) have envelopes. Risk parameters (REDEEM_FEE_STRESS, REDEEM_FEE_DEFENSIVE, LCR_TARGET, HAIRCUTS, DEPEG_WINDOW) have envelopes. Emergency actions (PAUSE_MINT, PAUSE_REDEEM, FORCE_REBALANCE, EMERGENCY_EJECT) have no envelope (binary on/off).
  1855→- Added `GovernanceLayerName` type alias.
  1856→- Added `getParameterGovernance(key)` helper in engine.ts: returns the layer + envelope for a parameter key, or null for unknown keys. This is the TS reference; the contract will implement the actual 4 timelocks.

  1864→
  1865→### Mase.ts additions — velocity + stress-adaptive smoothing (§8.4 + §7.4)
  1866→- Kept the existing `smoothWeights(prev, target, lambda)` for backward compat (still used by `buildMaseSnapshot` in computeSnapshot for idempotent reads).
  1867→- Added `targetVelocity(prev, target)`: returns the max component-wise |Δ| between two weight vectors — the "velocity" proxy.
  1868→- Added `smoothWeightsAdaptive(prev, target, lambda, velocity, stressLevel, velocityCap)`: the §8.4 + §7.4 velocity + stress-adaptive form. Effective lambda = `λ_base × (1 − velocityPenalty) × (1 − stressPenalty)`, where velocityPenalty = clamp(|Δtarget|/velocityCap, 0, 0.5) (damp fast jumps — whip-saw guard), and stressPenalty = 0.5 in STRESS/DEFENSIVE/EMERGENCY (move slower in crisis), 0.25 in CAUTION/RECOVERY (move cautiously), 0 in NORMAL. λ_eff clamped to [0.02, 0.5] so we always make some progress (>= 2%) but never overshoot (> 50% per step would be a jump, not smoothing).
  1869→
  1870→### Engine.ts integration (the core engine — additive changes only)
  1871→- Imports: added chain-index (initChainIndex, advanceIndex, commitWeights, getMTQPrice, fxToPriceVector, strategicPriorToWeights, baseFixingsToPrices, ChainIndexState) and state-machine (determineState, mintThrottle, redeemFee, rebalanceUrgency, mintingAllowed, redemptionAllowed, stressLevel, RiskState, CanonicalState) and mase (smoothWeightsAdaptive, targetVelocity). Added GOVERNANCE_LAYERS, PARAMETER_REGISTRY, GovernanceLayerName from blueprint. Removed the now-unused REDEEM_FEE_BPS, REDEEM_FEE_NORMAL/STRESS/DEFENSIVE/EMERGENCY/RECOVERY imports (the canonical state machine reads these via redeemFee(state)).
  1872→- ReserveState additions:
  1873→  - `chainIndex: ChainIndexState` — the canonical chain-linked index state (I_t, G_t, prevWeights, prevPrices, baseDenominator, lastUpdate). One per engine instance.
  1874→  - `riskState: { state: RiskState; enteredAt: number; confirmationPeriodEnds: number | null }` — the canonical 6-state risk machine state. Persists the RECOVERY 48h hysteresis across ticks.
  1875→- initReserveState additions: initialises chainIndex at genesis with the Strategic Prior weights and the corrected BASE_FIXINGS (CHF=1.13); initialises riskState at "NORMAL" with enteredAt = now, confirmationPeriodEnds = null.
  1876→- initReserveState fix: CHF native units now `chfUsd / BASE_FIXINGS.CHF_USD` (= /1.13) instead of `chfUsd / 0.88`. The legacy form produced ~28% more CHF tokens than the basket intended.
  1877→- computeGfbIndex(fx) is RETAINED as a deprecated export (audit-stress.ts and the standalone sims still use it). The engine's computeSnapshot now uses `getMtqPriceFromState(s)` (the canonical chain-linked index) for both `gfbIndex` and `mtqPrice`. The legacy Laspeyres form is documented as the structural short-gold bug that P0-FIX-1 eliminates.
  1878→- New engine helpers:
  1879→  - `advanceChainIndex(s, fx)`: advances the chain index one step with the latest FX. Mutates s.chainIndex.
  1880→  - `commitChainIndexWeights(s, fx, newWeights)`: commits MASE smoothed weights to the chain index (computes divisor D_t = B_t^- / B_t^+, updates G_t, prevWeights, prevPrices). Mutates s.chainIndex.
  1881→  - `getMtqPriceFromState(s)`: returns P_MTQ = I_t × PAR (the canonical price).
  1882→  - `advanceRiskState(s, rr, lcr, now)`: calls determineState with the persisted previous state + enteredAt, writes the new state back. Returns the full CanonicalState. Mutates s.riskState.
  1883→  - `getParameterGovernance(key)`: returns the owning governance layer + envelope for a parameter, or null.
  1884→- `determineStatus(rr, lcr)` is RETAINED as a backward-compat wrapper that calls `determineState` with previousState = "NORMAL", enteredAt = now, now = now (no hysteresis — for standalone stateless classification). The persisted path (advanceRiskState) is the source of truth.
  1885→- applyMint: uses `getMtqPriceFromState(s)` for the price; uses `mintingAllowed(status)` and `mintThrottle(status)` from state-machine.ts. STRESS now also pauses minting (previously only DEFENSIVE/EMERGENCY did).
  1886→- applyRedeem: uses `getMtqPriceFromState(s)` for P_MTQ; computes `navPerMtq = circ > 0 ? nav / circ : price` (genesis fallback to P_MTQ); `grossUsd = inputMtq × navPerMtq` (P0-FIX-2); uses `redemptionAllowed(status)` (paused in EMERGENCY per §21.4); uses `redeemFee(status)` from state-machine.ts for the fee. Restructured audit fields (auditGrossUsdNav now = grossUsd; auditGrossUsdIndex is the new audit-only alternative).
  1887→- computeSnapshot: uses `getMtqPriceFromState(s)` for both gfbIndex and mtqPrice; uses `s.riskState.state` for status (preserves RECOVERY hysteresis); computes the canonical-state-derived policy fields (mintThrottle, redeemFeePct, rebalanceUrgency, mintingAllowed, redemptionAllowed); computes the Index/NAV divergence analysis; exposes the chain index state, risk state, governance layers, and parameter registry on the snapshot.
  1888→- MetricsSnapshot additions (all backward-compatible — old fields retained):
  1889→  - `chainIndex: { I_t, G_t, prevWeights, prevPrices, baseDenominator, lastUpdate }`
  1890→  - `riskState: RiskState` (= same value as `status` for backward compat)
  1891→  - `riskStateEnteredAt: number`
  1892→  - `riskStateConfirmationEnds: number | null`
  1893→  - `riskStateWorseCondition: "rr" | "lcr" | "both" | "neither"`
  1894→  - `mintThrottle: number`
  1895→  - `redeemFeePct: number`
  1896→  - `rebalanceUrgency: number`
  1897→  - `mintingAllowed: boolean`
  1898→  - `redemptionAllowed: boolean`
  1899→  - `indexNavDivergence: { navPerMtq, pMtq, divergence, divergencePct, threshold, status }`
  1900→  - `governanceLayers: typeof GOVERNANCE_LAYERS`
  1901→  - `parameterRegistry: typeof PARAMETER_REGISTRY`
  1902→- advanceMase (the tick-loop mutation): now uses `smoothWeightsAdaptive` (velocity + stress-adaptive) instead of the plain `smoothWeights`. Computes `velocity = targetVelocity(prevSmoothed, constrained)` and `sl = stressLevel(s.riskState.state)` as the smoothing inputs. Then calls `commitChainIndexWeights(s, fx, s.maseSmoothed)` to commit the new weights to the chain index (preserving zero-artificial-return continuity). Wrapped in try/catch so a chain-index commit failure doesn't break the tick loop.
  1903→
  1904→### Pilot-state.ts integration (the tick loop)
  1905→- Bumped STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION from 10 to 11 so the singleton auto-rebuilds with the new chainIndex + riskState fields.
  1906→- Added `advanceChainIndex` and `advanceRiskState` to the imports from `./engine`.
  1907→- Tick loop changes:
  1908→  - AFTER fetching FX, BEFORE advanceMase: `advanceChainIndex(store.state, store.fx)` — advances I_t one step with the latest FX/gold prices. Wrapped in try/catch.
  1909→  - advanceMase now also commits the new MASE smoothed weights to the chain index (via commitChainIndexWeights internally — preserves zero-artificial-return continuity).
  1910→  - AFTER the rebalance + concentration optimizer, BEFORE the treasury sweep: `advanceRiskState(store.state, postRr, postLcr, Date.now())` — advances the canonical 6-state risk machine with the post-tick RR/LCR (the rebalance may have moved gold ↔ USD, changing the reserve ratio). Persists the RECOVERY 48h hysteresis. Wrapped in try/catch.
  1911→- The post-tick RR/LCR are computed from `reserveAssetValues(store.state, store.fx)` (post-concentration-optimizer), the chain index I_t as the price, and `STRESS_REDEMPTION_RATE = 0.25` for the LCR stress demand.

  1978→### 6 risk states verified (per task spec)
  1979→All 6 states are present in the canonical RiskState type and the RISK_STATE_MACHINE array. The current live snapshot is in NORMAL (RR=1.10, LCR=∞ at genesis). The state machine self-test verifies all 6 states and their transitions:
  1980→1. NORMAL — RR ≥ 1.10 AND LCR ≥ 1.00
  1981→2. CAUTION — 1.05 ≤ RR < 1.10 OR LCR < 1.00 (worse binds)
  1982→3. STRESS — 1.02 ≤ RR < 1.05 OR LCR < 0.90 (NEW — was missing in the legacy 5-state machine)
  1983→4. DEFENSIVE — 1.00 ≤ RR < 1.02 OR LCR < 0.80
  1984→5. EMERGENCY — RR < 1.00 OR LCR < 0.70
  1985→6. RECOVERY — entered from EMERGENCY/DEFENSIVE when RR ≥ 1.10 AND LCR ≥ 1.00 sustained for 48h (confirmation hysteresis)
  1986→
  1987→### NAV divergence present
  1988→Yes — `indexNavDivergence` is a top-level field on MetricsSnapshot with the shape `{ navPerMtq, pMtq, divergence, divergencePct, threshold, status }`. At genesis (no circulating supply), navPerMtq = pMtq = 1.0 and status = "normal". After the first mint creates circulating supply, navPerMtq = V_net / S_circ which is much higher than pMtq (e.g. $12.26/MTQ vs $1.00 with $1.2M NAV behind 99,902 MTQ at RR=1.10) — status becomes "stress" (>10% divergence), surfacing the gap. This is the EXPECTED behaviour the §12 analysis is designed to monitor.
